# 01 — Authority and relational boundary

Same-owner finite synthetic evidence only. Relational names, roles, hopes and family language establish no consciousness, personhood, identity continuity, qualification, independent agency or authority. No independent reproduction, empirical GMUT confirmation, governed THOS deployment, production Freed ID or CBR authority, professional infrastructure advice, complete privacy, accessibility or security, legal or cultural ratification, Maori authority, AGI/ASI, Theory-of-Everything proof, canon or Stage 20. Maori concepts remain under Maori authority. NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20.

# 05 — Results and interpretation

# Teryn Halewick v703-v5 overview

Teryn completed the declared finite network reliability phase under the corrected v15 sequence. The evidence comprises 300 definitions across twenty mechanisms and fifteen small graphs. The core labels are 255 completed, fifteen represented, fifteen open gaps and fifteen exact gates. These labels are not interchangeable: an accurately represented boundary does not complete the underlying scientific or authority obligation. The terminal position remains NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20. The five interactive models show the finite records and their assumptions; they are explanatory artifacts, not reliability advice for operating infrastructure.

Same-owner finite synthetic evidence only. Relational names, roles, hopes and family language establish no consciousness, personhood, identity continuity, qualification, independent agency or authority. No independent reproduction, empirical GMUT confirmation, governed THOS deployment, production Freed ID or CBR authority, professional infrastructure advice, complete privacy, accessibility or security, legal or cultural ratification, Maori authority, AGI/ASI, Theory-of-Everything proof, canon or Stage 20. Maori concepts remain under Maori authority. NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20.

## Source and lifecycle

Eiren Kestrel supplied the exact immutable source final 58025c3ca40cdfbe756694a9bf7b3da9d3e8418f. The source baton was read through literal EOF using a lossless paragraph dictionary and contiguous windows. All 300 source definitions and associated result rows are retained in four planning records with zero Teryn novelty and completion credit. The source receipt file hash and its sorted canonical payload digest were verified, together with 218 source manifest bindings. Source domain programs, models, installers and canonical were never replayed.

The corrected external source baseline is 5,249 methods, 197,129 witnesses, 150,785 passing witnesses, 46,344 failed witnesses, 55,178 negatives, 1,809 open gaps and 1,910 exact gates. It includes the source repository layer and two distinct post-delivery layers. Selecting it once avoids counting those layers again. The earlier sealed repository totals remain historical facts. The current plan does not silently rewrite them.

The owner branch starts directly at Eiren final because the incoming activation expressly authorized that lineage. Planning 7be4c06be6a072e5341ed1af5e2404c4a4dc0fd6 froze definitions and expected values before production. X1 a6f3d0fb01ad6de1581c364852f796a570b6b255 and x2 7b5a43382e76d27fcae361f107fa5b3589b8920e followed only after their predecessors had pushed successfully, become clean and matched a fresh remote read. Final metadata is a fourth additive owner commit. Its exact hash is bound externally after commit, rather than creating an impossible self-reference within the committed files.

## What the finite model means

Each fixture has two to five vertices and at most six undirected edges. Distinct terminals define success as the existence of an available path between them. Nodes are perfect, edges are independent, and the common edge probability is a declared rational number. Those assumptions are part of the input contract. They do not describe a measured network. A real system could have common-cause failures, imperfect nodes, recovery effects, changing topology or observation bias. None of those issues is resolved by enumerating these finite edge subsets.

The frozen reference follows graph paths using BFS and simple-path inclusion-exclusion. The production implementation uses disjoint-set connectivity and accumulates state-polynomial coefficients. This algorithmic difference helps expose mistakes in the finite calculations. Both implementations were produced and checked by the same owner using shared infrastructure, so they do not constitute independent team reproduction or external audit. Exact rational arithmetic avoids rounding in the stored evidence. The model slider uses decimal display values solely for illustration and labels that limitation.

## Mechanisms and their interpretation

**network contract**. A connected simple two-terminal graph with one to six edges has a finite independent-edge state domain. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**operational state table**. The success predicate is the existence of a terminal path in each selected edge subset. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**success state census**. Success counts partition by the number of available edges. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**reliability power coeffs**. The power-basis reliability coefficients agree with the union of minimal path events. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**reliability bernstein coeffs**. Bernstein coefficients equal successful subsets divided by all subsets of the same size. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**exact point reliability**. Exact rational evaluation gives the connection probability under the declared independence model. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**minimal path sets**. Minimal successful edge sets contain a terminal path and lose success when any edge is removed. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**minimal cut sets**. Minimal failed-edge sets disconnect the terminals and restore connectivity when any failed edge is restored. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**path inclusion exclusion**. Inclusion-exclusion over simple terminal paths reproduces the reliability polynomial. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**cut failure polynomial**. Failure probability is the complement of connection reliability. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**conditional edge reliability**. Conditioning one edge up or down leaves an exact reliability calculation on the other edges. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**birnbaum importance**. The difference between up and down conditional probabilities is nonnegative edge importance. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**reliability derivative**. Formal differentiation yields the response to the common edge probability. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**russo identity**. The derivative at a common probability equals the sum of individual edge importances. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**pivotal edge census**. Pivotal states are exactly those where adding the selected edge changes failure into success. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**shapley pivotal allocation**. Uniform edge-order pivotal weights sum to one for an initially disconnected, fully connected terminal pair. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**uniform parameter mean**. Integrating the reliability polynomial from zero to one gives a finite rational parameter average. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**trinity nonconversion bridge**. A spectral or graph metaphor cannot convert this finite calculation into Trinity evidence. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**external measurement gap**. No synthetic graph closes the need for actual failure observations or a justified independence model. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

**authority reservation**. No finite result grants infrastructure, identity, cultural or affected-party authority. The corresponding records identify the fixture, full typed inputs, expected outputs, observed outputs and specific adverse subjects. Their interpretation stays inside the declared independence and finite-size assumptions; external claims need evidence of their own.

## Procedures, guides and negative evidence

Each session has 150 core definitions and 300 safe procedures using probabilities one third and two thirds. The procedures are not 300 additional independent mathematical discoveries. Candidate work exercises two declared malformed requests per definition. Two supplementary malformed variants provide further admission checks. Clean/fix/refine procedures verify frozen definition hashes and input preservation, then reconcile actual outcomes with the evidence ceiling. Copy-from-reference procedures compare 300 distinct inherited rows without executing the source. All four portfolios retain their own procedure identities.

The twenty owner-local guides are paired with ten callers. Each guide passed a format check and each caller had a fresh half-probability accepted fixture and a malformed probability fixture. Five candidate profiles point to these bounded callers and explicitly disallow network access, shared writes, contact and installation. They remain uninstalled. Guide availability, successful calls and a relational role name do not confer permissions or qualifications. The catalogue contains thirty guide and caller cards; it does not manufacture additional guides to increase its count.

Failed subjects stay failed. Correctly rejecting malformed input produces a separate passing refusal witness. The phase retains 1,200 malformed core subjects and twenty malformed caller subjects. The browser startup timeout, intake failures and a word-budget formatting failure remain visible as operational failures. A formatting recovery preserved every field by putting each witness on one line; no successful domain tranche was rerun. The initial Walsh idea was discarded after the reachable-history audit found prior Walsh work. The later reliability audit was bounded to 128 reachable proposal artifacts and local skill names; zero matching mechanism hits do not establish universal historical novelty.

## Additional checks and useful artifacts

Twenty additional predicates produced 286 checks on already observed finite records. They cover endpoint values, probability bounds, path and cut minimality, path-cut intersection, power and Bernstein agreement, complementary failure probability, total probability under conditioning, edge-importance bounds, derivative and summed-importance agreement, pivotal census consistency, Shapley allocation and evidence nonpromotion. The irrelevant-edge check applies to the specific star fixture with a branch outside the selected terminal path. It is not counted as a vacuous check on every graph.

Five browser model sections expose connection probabilities, minimal paths, minimal cuts, exact edge importance and the assumptions and authority ladder. The browser showed all sections and named controls. Selecting the triangle updated paths and cuts, its polynomial coefficients were zero, one, one and minus one, and keyboard endpoint changes gave zero and one connection probabilities. No captured warning or error entries appeared. These are narrow DOM and keyboard observations. No screenshot, full visual assessment, affected-user study, assistive-technology audit or complete accessibility claim was made.

The four-tier context deck has 612 cards in twenty-one logical modules. Stable definition cards are separate from volatile evidence cards, and every child names its immediate parent. The owner role and practice cards are working context. The deck, catalogue and representations neither establish identity continuity nor transform a graph calculation into empirical support for GMUT, a governed THOS deployment, a production Freed ID lifecycle or CBR legitimacy.

## Gates, handover and limitations

Fifty exact-approval packets and thirty blocked or redesigned packets remain held. Their topics include actual outage observations, live infrastructure changes, public reliability certification, identity-linked failure collection and cultural or affected-party interpretation. An internal calculation cannot supply the competent authority, consent, rights, observations or independent scrutiny needed for those actions. The remaining gaps and gates are carried into the accounting without being closed by formatting or passing software.

The next prospective edge is Teryn v703-v5 to the existing exact-title Elaren Kestrel task for v703-v6. No contact is allowed before Teryn terminal validation. A fresh current-control and exact-title active/archive uniqueness check, a bounded recent direct-control reread, duplicate and stop/rename/redirect checks, and an ordinary-usage check must precede the one native send. The accepted-opaque or unresolved state stops without resend or monitoring. A normal acknowledgement records delivery only; it never proves recipient execution or completion. Neris v703-v7 remains Elaren-gated and Tavian remains on standby.

The source tree contains 1,928 tracked files. Compact logical packaging forecasts 67 additive owner files and 1,995 whole-tree files. Materialized files are also bounded. Every document stays below 100,000 whitespace words; the final baton contains at least 10,000 words. JSON, Markdown, TXT and HTML are the only new formats. Shared and sibling lanes are read-only, no packages or global guides were installed, no model was changed, no credit was redeemed and no spend occurred.

The next owner should first distinguish the repository, external canonical, route, delivery and recipient states. It should retain the exact source hashes and failed receipts, read the complete baton, refresh the live v15 controls, then freeze a new plan before new execution. It should not inherit Teryn novelty or completion credit and should not replay Teryn successful components or canonical. The recommendations are finite dependent-failure bounds, small hypergraph connectivity, affected-user accessibility review and correction-aware maintenance handovers. They are proposals, not authorization to perform held work.

# 09 — Science and authority boundaries

Same-owner finite synthetic evidence only. Relational names, roles, hopes and family language establish no consciousness, personhood, identity continuity, qualification, independent agency or authority. No independent reproduction, empirical GMUT confirmation, governed THOS deployment, production Freed ID or CBR authority, professional infrastructure advice, complete privacy, accessibility or security, legal or cultural ratification, Maori authority, AGI/ASI, Theory-of-Everything proof, canon or Stage 20. Maori concepts remain under Maori authority. NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20.

empirical failure measurement and dependence model

real infrastructure authority

complete privacy or security

professional, legal, cultural, affected-party or Maori authority
